**RESOLVED (2026-10-17 17:46) — deploy-hygiene drift (running service was on a DELETED/replaced standalone).** Cause: an ODG convergence mission ran a bare `next build` mid-loop (16:54, BUILD_ID `4feQN9lhyXnihNojlKhH2`) that replaced `.next/standalone` WITHOUT the documented static-copy step (`DEPLOYMENT.md:104-110`), so `odg-preprod` (PID 2940100) ran from deleted inodes AND the on-disk standalone was **incomplete** (`.next/static` missing — a restart onto it would have served a broken dashboard; caught by a precondition check). **Resolution (CTO-authorized controlled restart; existing procedure; loopback-only, no TLS/DNS/network change):** completed the standalone via the documented `cp -r .next/static .next/standalone/.next/ && cp -r public .next/standalone/` (the repo `.next/static` was present + BUILD_ID-coherent `4feQN9`, built 16:54 from dashboard source unchanged since `307a456`), then `sudo systemctl restart odg-preprod`. **Post-restart VERIFIED:** new PID **3035336**, cwd `= .next/standalone` (NOT `(deleted)`), `/`+`/api/health`+`/api/runtime-state` = **200**, real served chunk `/_next/static/chunks/*.js` = **200** (static OK, not broken), health `{status:ready,storage:READY}`, runtime-state real (`generatedAt`, keys health/missions/autonomy/queue/capabilities), **running standalone BUILD_ID == `.next` == `4feQN9`**, and `runtime-identity-check` → **CONSISTENT (EXIT 0)**. **NEW CAPABILITY:** `scripts/runtime-identity-check.{js,test.js}` — pure fail-closed detector (on-disk coherence + deleted-inode signal + served-vs-disk when exposed) + live wrapper; **8/8 unit EXIT 0**, in the canonical `npm test` glob; it detected the live DRIFT (exit 3) pre-fix and reports CONSISTENT (exit 0) post-fix. **CM-2 DONE (2026-10-17 17:54):** `/api/health` now exposes `buildId` (memoized; prefers `ODG_DEPLOYED_BUILD_ID` env, else reads the standalone's `.next/BUILD_ID` once; fail-closed to null — no secret/path). Deployed via `npm run build` (EXIT 0, TS 11.4s, 6 canonical routes) + documented `cp` + restart (PID 3036114). Served `buildId="vF3XU3UcWyAfeCi_vMDeJ"` == on-disk; `runtime-identity-check` → **CONSISTENT via reliable served==disk match** (EXIT 0). Locked health-shape test updated to the 4-key contract (strict). Rollback: systemd `Restart=on-failure` + rebuild-from-HEAD (`npm run build` + cp) + §M-6 vault.
